{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NXjanTtB2iaUVc4kfQ1UBX","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Foothold Technology, Inc. — Product Research\n     2→\n     3→Researched: 2026-02-15\n     4→Developer website: http://footholdtechnology.com\n     5→\n     6→## Overview\n     7→\n     8→Foothold Technology, Inc. is a New York City-based software company that builds electronic health record and case management software for the **human services and behavioral health** sector. Founded in 2000 by three social service agencies that needed case management software, Foothold has grown to serve over 1,000 provider agencies across 28 states plus Guam and Puerto Rico. The company has a particularly strong presence in New York and New Jersey, where it serves more agencies than any other EHR vendor in the behavioral health/human services space.\n     9→\n    10→In 2017, Foothold was acquired by **Alpine Software Group (ASG)**, a division of private equity firm Alpine Investors. In July 2021, ASG launched **Radicle Health** as an umbrella brand grouping several human services software companies: Foothold Technology (behavioral health EHR + HMIS), Exym (behavioral health EHR, California-focused), KCare/extendedReach (child & family welfare), Link2Feed (food bank/poverty relief), SaraWorks (client communication, joined 2024), and Stabilify (child welfare). Tyler Hoffman serves as CEO of both Foothold Technology and as Group CEO of Radicle Health. The CHPL contact email (jpalmer@radicle-health.com) confirms the corporate relationship. Each company maintains its own brand and product under the Radicle Health umbrella. This is a classic PE buy-and-build strategy in vertical human services software.\n    11→\n    12→Foothold was formerly a Certified B Corporation and has described itself as achieving \"one of the highest scores ever seen\" in HIPAA security testing. Estimated annual revenue is in the $5–25M range (~$15.8M per one estimate). The company has approximately 36–200 employees (sources vary, likely reflecting growth under Radicle Health).\n    13→\n    14→## Product: AWARDS\n    15→\n    16→CHPL ID: 9267 (`15.04.04.1500.AWAR.03.00.1.171220`)\n    17→\n    18→### What It Is\n    19→\n    20→AWARDS is a **web-based EHR and case management system purpose-built for nonprofit human services and behavioral health organizations**. It is not a traditional ambulatory or hospital EHR — its primary users are community-based behavioral health providers, homeless services agencies, residential programs, and developmental disability service providers. A key distinguishing feature: AWARDS is described as the **only record-keeping system federally certified as both a Behavioral Health EHR and a Homeless Management Information System (HMIS)** — the latter being a HUD-mandated data system for homeless services.\n    21→\n    22→The certified product is the full AWARDS platform — it is not a module of a larger system. However, some features (e-prescribing, e-labs, business intelligence) are sold as add-on modules. The product is cloud/SaaS-hosted (the help center is on Zendesk at awards1.zendesk.com, and a demo database domain demodb.footholdtechnology.com was observed).\n    23→\n    24→The ONC certification is broad, covering clinical data capabilities (a)(1)–(a)(5), (a)(12), (a)(14), transitions of care (b)(1)–(b)(3), patient portal (e)(1), FHIR API (g)(7), (g)(9), (g)(10), clinical quality measures (c)(1)–(c)(3), immunization registry reporting (h)(1), and a full security suite. This breadth reflects a product that spans clinical, administrative, and interoperability functions.\n    25→\n    26→### Users & Market\n    27→\n    28→**Target users**: Staff at nonprofit human services agencies — clinicians (psychiatrists, therapists, social workers, counselors), case managers, program directors, billing staff, administrative personnel, and agency leadership. The SED intended user description is simply \"Varied,\" reflecting the breadth of user roles.\n    29→\n    30→**Clinical settings and service verticals**:\n    31→- Mental health (outpatient, residential, community-based)\n    32→- Alcohol and substance use disorder treatment\n    33→- Homeless services and supportive housing\n    34→- Developmental disabilities / intellectual disabilities (I/DD)\n    35→- Home and Community Based Services (HCBS)\n    36→- Day habilitation programs\n    37→- Supported employment services\n    38→- Youth and family services\n    39→- Senior services\n    40→- Employment and training programs\n    41→- Veterans services\n    42→\n    43→**Scale**: 1,000+ provider agencies across 28 states plus territories. The HMIS module alone is deployed at 50+ HUD Continua of Care around the country. Strongest presence in New York and New Jersey.\n    44→\n    45→**Notable customers/case studies**:\n    46→- **BRC** (NYC homelessness outreach — 7,500 monthly contacts)\n    47→- **Hudson Guild** (NYC mental health services)\n    48→- **MHY Family Services**\n    49→- **NYC Department of Homelessness** (HMIS deployments)\n    50→- **Goodwill Industries** (referenced in search results)\n    51→- **Mental Health Association of Columbia-Greene Counties, NY**\n    52→- **CARES of NY** (collaborative HMIS)\n    53→\n    54→**Competitive landscape**: Competes with Netsmart, Qualifacts/Credible, Therapy Partner, and other behavioral health EHR vendors. AWARDS' differentiator is its dual EHR + HMIS certification and its focus on multi-program human services agencies rather than clinical-only behavioral health practices.\n    55→\n    56→### Modules & Functionality\n    57→\n    58→The following modules and features were found described across vendor materials, product pages, customer case studies, and third-party listings:\n    59→\n    60→**Core Clinical/Case Management**:\n    61→- **Treatment Plans & Case Management**: Multi-service, connected caregiving coordination across programs. Supports treatment planning workflows for behavioral health and human services.\n    62→- **Service Documentation**: Workflow-consistent clinical documentation for staff, tied to specific services and programs.\n    63→- **Progress Notes**: Clinical progress notes with customizable templates.\n    64→- **Intake & Assessment**: Patient health status determination, medical history, conditions. Supports intake workflows for new clients/consumers.\n    65→- **Medication Management**: Medication administration records (MARs), medication history, medication allergy tracking, alerts.\n    66→- **Vital Signs**: Clinical vital signs tracking.\n    67→- **Problem Lists**: Diagnosis and problem tracking (certified (a)(3)).\n    68→- **Demographics**: Patient/client demographics capture (certified (a)(5)).\n    69→- **Family Health History**: Certified (a)(12).\n    70→\n    71→**HMIS Module** (HUD-Compliant):\n    72→- Client demographics and contact information\n    73→- Housing status and history\n    74→- Homelessness episodes tracking\n    75→- Services provided documentation\n    76→- Income and benefits tracking\n    77→- Health insurance status\n    78→- Disabilities documentation\n    79→- Domestic violence indicators\n    80→- Program entry/exit data\n    81→- HUD Universal Data Elements\n    82→- CoC, ESG, PATH, SSVF reporting compliance\n    83→\n    84→**Administrative**:\n    85→- **Billing Integration**: Billing tied directly to service documentation; automated billing workflows. This is built into the core platform, not a separate product.\n    86→- **Facilities Maintenance**: Building/property management for residential programs — a module reflecting AWARDS' focus on residential and housing providers.\n    87→- **Human Resources**: Staff management and HR functions.\n    88→- **Scheduling & Alerts**: Calendar events, task tracking, color-coded alerts.\n    89→- **Audit Reports**: Compliance and audit trail reporting.\n    90→\n    91→**Reporting & Analytics**:\n    92→- **ReportBuilder**: Custom ad-hoc reports using any data field in the system.\n    93→- **DataBridge**: Direct database querying capability for ad-hoc reporting.\n    94→- **InSights**: Business intelligence add-on for advanced analytics.\n    95→- **100+ pre-built reports** geared to behavioral health and I/DD providers.\n    96→- **ExportBuilders**: Data export functionality (this is the mechanism referenced in the EHI export documentation URL).\n    97→\n    98→**Configurable Tools**:\n    99→- **FormBuilder**: Agencies can build custom forms and attach them to intake, discharge, progress notes, and other workflows. This is significant — it means AWARDS stores agency-defined custom data structures beyond its standard fields.\n   100→- **Configurable workflows**: Programs can be set up independently or globally across agencies.\n   101→\n   102→**Add-On Modules/Services**:\n   103→- **E-Prescribing**: Electronic prescribing including controlled substances (EPCS), with IMO (Intelligent Medical Objects) integration for medication coding.\n   104→- **E-Labs**: Electronic lab orders to LabCorp, Quest, and hundreds of other labs; results integration back into the chart.\n   105→- **Patient Portal**: Patient-facing access to health information (certified under (e)(1)). Limited details found on the portal's specific functionality.\n   106→\n   107→**Interoperability**:\n   108→- **Health Information Exchange (HIE)** participation via IHE-certified protocols.\n   109→- **C-CDA document creation and reconciliation** (certified (b)(1)–(b)(3)).\n   110→- **FHIR API** (certified (g)(7), (g)(9), (g)(10)). No public developer documentation or API endpoint details were found.\n   111→- **Immunization registry reporting** (certified (h)(1)).\n   112→- **Clinical quality measure recording, export, and reporting** (certified (c)(1)–(c)(3)).\n   113→- Partnership with **IMSNY** (Interoperability Collaborative for Managed Service in New York) for behavioral health data sharing.\n   114→- Integration with **Hixny** (New York state HIE).\n   115→- InterSystems-based interoperability engine.\n   116→\n   117→Sources: Foothold Technology website (footholdtechnology.com), product detail pages on resources.footholdtechnology.com, Alpine SG portfolio page, Capterra/GetApp search snippets, press releases, and HMIS proposal documents.\n   118→\n   119→### Data & Content\n   120→\n   121→Based on described features, certified criteria, and observed modules, AWARDS manages the following categories of data:\n   122→\n   123→**Clinical health records**:\n   124→- Patient/client demographics and e-prescribing module)\n   129→- Lab orders and lab results (via e-labs module)\n   130→- Vital signs\n   131→- Clinical progress notes\n   132→- Treatment plans\n   133→- Assessment data (intake, ongoing, discharge)\n   134→- Immunization records\n   135→- Family health history\n   136→- Clinical quality measure data\n   137→\n   138→**Case management and program data**:\n   139→- Service documentation (tied to specific programs and billing)\n   140→- Program enrollment, participation, and discharge records\n   141→- Scheduling and calendar events\n   142→- Multi-program coordination data (clients may be enrolled in multiple programs simultaneously)\n   143→\n   144→**HMIS/housing data** (HUD-mandated elements):\n   145→- Housing status and history\n   146→- Homelessness episodes\n   147→- Income and benefits information\n   148→- Health insurance status\n   149→- Disability information\n   150→- Domestic violence indicators\n   151→- HUD Universal Data Elements (a standardized set of ~30+ data fields mandated by HUD)\n   152→\n   153→**Administrative and operational data**:\n   154→- Billing records and claims (integrated with service documentation)\n   155→- Staff/HR records\n   156→- Facility maintenance records (for residential programs)\n   157→- Audit trails and compliance logs\n   158→\n   159→**Custom/agency-defined data**:\n   160→- Custom forms built via FormBuilder (these can contain any fields the agency defines)\n   161→- Custom assessments and instruments\n   162→\n   163→**Interoperability and exchange data**:\n   164→- C-CDA documents (sent and received)\n   165→- HIE exchange records\n   166→- FHIR resources (US Core profiles per (g)(10))\n   167→\n   168→**Reporting data**:\n   169→- Saved reports and report configurations\n   170→- DataBridge queries\n   171→- ExportBuilder configurations and exports\n   172→\n   173→**Key observations for EHI export assessment**:\n   174→1. The **FormBuilder** custom forms are a significant data source — agencies can define arbitrary data structures, and all that data would need to be exportable.\n   175→2. The **HMIS data** is distinctive to this product — housing status, homelessness episodes, income, benefits, domestic violence indicators are not standard EHR data fields and may or may not be covered by standard export mechanisms.\n   176→3. The **facilities maintenance** module stores data about buildings/properties, which is unusual for an EHR.\n   177→4. **Billing is integrated**, not separate — so billing/claims data is part of the product and should be in scope for EHI export.\n   178→5. The product serves clients enrolled in **multiple programs simultaneously**, so program-level data structures may be complex.\n   179→\n   180→**Gaps in research**:\n   181→- The Foothold Technology website is heavily JavaScript-rendered, making direct content extraction from most pages difficult. Search engine snippets and cached content provided the bulk of useful information.\n   182→- No detailed description of the **Patient Portal's** specific features was found beyond the fact that it exists and is certified for (e)(1).\n   183→- No public **FHIR API developer documentation** was found.\n   184→- **Pricing** is not publicly disclosed.\n   185→- The specific data model and database structure are not documented publicly.\n   186→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Founded in 2000 by three social service agencies that needed case management software, Foothold has grown to serve over 1,000 provider agencies across 28 states plus Guam and Puerto Rico. The company has a particularly strong presence in New York and New Jersey, where it serves more agencies than any other EHR vendor in the behavioral health/human services space.\n\nIn 2017, Foothold was acquired by **Alpine Software Group (ASG)**, a division of private equity firm Alpine Investors. In July 2021, ASG launched **Radicle Health** as an umbrella brand grouping several human services software companies: Foothold Technology (behavioral health EHR + HMIS), Exym (behavioral health EHR, California-focused), KCare/extendedReach (child & family welfare), Link2Feed (food bank/poverty relief), SaraWorks (client communication, joined 2024), and Stabilify (child welfare). Tyler Hoffman serves as CEO of both Foothold Technology and as Group CEO of Radicle Health. The CHPL contact email (jpalmer@radicle-health.com) confirms the corporate relationship. Each company maintains its own brand and product under the Radicle Health umbrella. This is a classic PE buy-and-build strategy in vertical human services software.\n\nFoothold was formerly a Certified B Corporation and has described itself as achieving \"one of the highest scores ever seen\" in HIPAA security testing. Estimated annual revenue is in the $5–25M range (~$15.8M per one estimate). The company has approximately 36–200 employees (sources vary, likely reflecting growth under Radicle Health).\n\n## Product: AWARDS\n\nCHPL ID: 9267 (`15.04.04.1500.AWAR.03.00.1.171220`)\n\n### What It Is\n\nAWARDS is a **web-based EHR and case management system purpose-built for nonprofit human services and behavioral health organizations**. It is not a traditional ambulatory or hospital EHR — its primary users are community-based behavioral health providers, homeless services agencies, residential programs, and developmental disability service providers. A key distinguishing feature: AWARDS is described as the **only record-keeping system federally certified as both a Behavioral Health EHR and a Homeless Management Information System (HMIS)** — the latter being a HUD-mandated data system for homeless services.\n\nThe certified product is the full AWARDS platform — it is not a module of a larger system. However, some features (e-prescribing, e-labs, business intelligence) are sold as add-on modules. The product is cloud/SaaS-hosted (the help center is on Zendesk at awards1.zendesk.com, and a demo database domain demodb.footholdtechnology.com was observed).\n\nThe ONC certification is broad, covering clinical data capabilities (a)(1)–(a)(5), (a)(12), (a)(14), transitions of care (b)(1)–(b)(3), patient portal (e)(1), FHIR API (g)(7), (g)(9), (g)(10), clinical quality measures (c)(1)–(c)(3), immunization registry reporting (h)(1), and a full security suite. This breadth reflects a product that spans clinical, administrative, and interoperability functions.\n\n### Users & Market\n\n**Target users**: Staff at nonprofit human services agencies — clinicians (psychiatrists, therapists, social workers, counselors), case managers, program directors, billing staff, administrative personnel, and agency leadership. The SED intended user description is simply \"Varied,\" reflecting the breadth of user roles.\n\n**Clinical settings and service verticals**:\n- Mental health (outpatient, residential, community-based)\n- Alcohol and substance use disorder treatment\n- Homeless services and supportive housing\n- Developmental disabilities / intellectual disabilities (I/DD)\n- Home and Community Based Services (HCBS)\n- Day habilitation programs\n- Supported employment services\n- Youth and family services\n- Senior services\n- Employment and training programs\n- Veterans services\n\n**Scale**: 1,000+ provider agencies across 28 states plus territories. The HMIS module alone is deployed at 50+ HUD Continua of Care around the country. Strongest presence in New York and New Jersey.\n\n**Notable customers/case studies**:\n- **BRC** (NYC homelessness outreach — 7,500 monthly contacts)\n- **Hudson Guild** (NYC mental health services)\n- **MHY Family Services**\n- **NYC Department of Homelessness** (HMIS deployments)\n- **Goodwill Industries** (referenced in search results)\n- **Mental Health Association of Columbia-Greene Counties, NY**\n- **CARES of NY** (collaborative HMIS)\n\n**Competitive landscape**: Competes with Netsmart, Qualifacts/Credible, Therapy Partner, and other behavioral health EHR vendors. AWARDS' differentiator is its dual EHR + HMIS certification and its focus on multi-program human services agencies rather than clinical-only behavioral health practices.\n\n### Modules & Functionality\n\nThe following modules and features were found described across vendor materials, product pages, customer case studies, and third-party listings:\n\n**Core Clinical/Case Management**:\n- **Treatment Plans & Case Management**: Multi-service, connected caregiving coordination across programs. Supports treatment planning workflows for behavioral health and human services.\n- **Service Documentation**: Workflow-consistent clinical documentation for staff, tied to specific services and programs.\n- **Progress Notes**: Clinical progress notes with customizable templates.\n- **Intake & Assessment**: Patient health status determination, medical history, conditions. Supports intake workflows for new clients/consumers.\n- **Medication Management**: Medication administration records (MARs), medication history, medication allergy tracking, alerts.\n- **Vital Signs**: Clinical vital signs tracking.\n- **Problem Lists**: Diagnosis and problem tracking (certified (a)(3)).\n- **Demographics**: Patient/client demographics capture (certified (a)(5)).\n- **Family Health History**: Certified (a)(12).\n\n**HMIS Module** (HUD-Compliant):\n- Client demographics and contact information\n- Housing status and history\n- Homelessness episodes tracking\n- Services provided documentation\n- Income and benefits tracking\n- Health insurance status\n- Disabilities documentation\n- Domestic violence indicators\n- Program entry/exit data\n- HUD Universal Data Elements\n- CoC, ESG, PATH, SSVF reporting compliance\n\n**Administrative**:\n- **Billing Integration**: Billing tied directly to service documentation; automated billing workflows. This is built into the core platform, not a separate product.\n- **Facilities Maintenance**: Building/property management for residential programs — a module reflecting AWARDS' focus on residential and housing providers.\n- **Human Resources**: Staff management and HR functions.\n- **Scheduling & Alerts**: Calendar events, task tracking, color-coded alerts.\n- **Audit Reports**: Compliance and audit trail reporting.\n\n**Reporting & Analytics**:\n- **ReportBuilder**: Custom ad-hoc reports using any data field in the system.\n- **DataBridge**: Direct database querying capability for ad-hoc reporting.\n- **InSights**: Business intelligence add-on for advanced analytics.\n- **100+ pre-built reports** geared to behavioral health and I/DD providers.\n- **ExportBuilders**: Data export functionality (this is the mechanism referenced in the EHI export documentation URL).\n\n**Configurable Tools**:\n- **FormBuilder**: Agencies can build custom forms and attach them to intake, discharge, progress notes, and other workflows. This is significant — it means AWARDS stores agency-defined custom data structures beyond its standard fields.\n- **Configurable workflows**: Programs can be set up independently or globally across agencies.\n\n**Add-On Modules/Services**:\n- **E-Prescribing**: Electronic prescribing including controlled substances (EPCS), with IMO (Intelligent Medical Objects) integration for medication coding.\n- **E-Labs**: Electronic lab orders to LabCorp, Quest, and hundreds of other labs; results integration back into the chart.\n- **Patient Portal**: Patient-facing access to health information (certified under (e)(1)). Limited details found on the portal's specific functionality.\n\n**Interoperability**:\n- **Health Information Exchange (HIE)** participation via IHE-certified protocols.\n- **C-CDA document creation and reconciliation** (certified (b)(1)–(b)(3)).\n- **FHIR API** (certified (g)(7), (g)(9), (g)(10)). No public developer documentation or API endpoint details were found.\n- **Immunization registry reporting** (certified (h)(1)).\n- **Clinical quality measure recording, export, and reporting** (certified (c)(1)–(c)(3)).\n- Partnership with **IMSNY** (Interoperability Collaborative for Managed Service in New York) for behavioral health data sharing.\n- Integration with **Hixny** (New York state HIE).\n- InterSystems-based interoperability engine.\n\nSources: Foothold Technology website (footholdtechnology.com), product detail pages on resources.footholdtechnology.com, Alpine SG portfolio page, Capterra/GetApp search snippets, press releases, and HMIS proposal documents.\n\n### Data & Content\n\nBased on described features, certified criteria, and observed modules, AWARDS manages the following categories of data:\n\n**Clinical health records**:\n- Patient/client demographics and contact information\n- Problem lists / diagnoses\n- Medication lists and medication allergy lists\n- Medication administration records (MARs)\n- Prescription data (via e-prescribing module)\n- Lab orders and lab results (via e-labs module)\n- Vital signs\n- Clinical progress notes\n- Treatment plans\n- Assessment data (intake, ongoing, discharge)\n- Immunization records\n- Family health history\n- Clinical quality measure data\n\n**Case management and program data**:\n- Service documentation (tied to specific programs and billing)\n- Program enrollment, participation, and discharge records\n- Scheduling and calendar events\n- Multi-program coordination data (clients may be enrolled in multiple programs simultaneously)\n\n**HMIS/housing data** (HUD-mandated elements):\n- Housing status and history\n- Homelessness episodes\n- Income and benefits information\n- Health insurance status\n- Disability information\n- Domestic violence indicators\n- HUD Universal Data Elements (a standardized set of ~30+ data fields mandated by HUD)\n\n**Administrative and operational data**:\n- Billing records and claims (integrated with service documentation)\n- Staff/HR records\n- Facility maintenance records (for residential programs)\n- Audit trails and compliance logs\n\n**Custom/agency-defined data**:\n- Custom forms built via FormBuilder (these can contain any fields the agency defines)\n- Custom assessments and instruments\n\n**Interoperability and exchange data**:\n- C-CDA documents (sent and received)\n- HIE exchange records\n- FHIR resources (US Core profiles per (g)(10))\n\n**Reporting data**:\n- Saved reports and report configurations\n- DataBridge queries\n- ExportBuilder configurations and exports\n\n**Key observations for EHI export assessment**:\n1. The **FormBuilder** custom forms are a significant data source — agencies can define arbitrary data structures, and all that data would need to be exportable.\n2. The **HMIS data** is distinctive to this product — housing status, homelessness episodes, income, benefits, domestic violence indicators are not standard EHR data fields and may or may not be covered by standard export mechanisms.\n3. The **facilities maintenance** module stores data about buildings/properties, which is unusual for an EHR.\n4. **Billing is integrated**, not separate — so billing/claims data is part of the product and should be in scope for EHI export.\n5. The product serves clients enrolled in **multiple programs simultaneously**, so program-level data structures may be complex.\n\n**Gaps in research**:\n- The Foothold Technology website is heavily JavaScript-rendered, making direct content extraction from most pages difficult. Search engine snippets and cached content provided the bulk of useful information.\n- No detailed description of the **Patient Portal's** specific features was found beyond the fact that it exists and is certified for (e)(1).\n- No public **FHIR API developer documentation** was found.\n- **Pricing** is not publicly disclosed.\n- The specific data model and database structure are not documented publicly.\n","numLines":186,"startLine":1,"totalLines":186}}}

CSV, TXT, XLS, or XML.  Exported files can be saved to your computer, then uploaded into other data collection systems and/or worked with in other applications as needed.  Additionally, as can be done with ReportBuilders, ExportBuilder formats can be saved and shared for future use.\n\n\nEach ExportBuilders is located within a corresponding ReportBuilder, accessible via the related AWARDS module or from the \nReports\n module.  Upon accessing a ReportBuilder users see a \nProvide ExportBuilder Options\n checkbox at the top of the initial ReportBuilder settings page.  Checking off this option updates the page (and those that follow) to recognize that you will be using the ExportBuilder components for that feature rather than the basic ReportBuilder.\n\n\nIMPORTANT! The ExportBuilder functionality was designed for use by individuals who are familiar with export files and formats, their design, and setup.  It is not intended for use by all AWARDS users.\n\n\n\n\nRequired Permissions\n\n\nUse of ExportBuilders in AWARDS requires the following permissions:\n\n\n\n\n\n\nProgram Chart Access\n\n\n\n\n\n\nSave ReportBuilder Formats For All Staff\n \n(optional - required to save ExportBuilder formats for all users rather than for just oneself)\n\n\n\n\n\n\nIn addition, which ReportBuilders/ExportBuilders are accessible to you is based on your permissions for functionality elsewhere in the system.  For example, you will not have access to Intake reports if you do not have access to the Intake/Admission module functionality.\n\n\nPermissions are assigned using the \nPermissions Maintenance\n feature.  If you do not have access to that feature and need a permission listed here, please contact your supervisor or your local Help Desk for assistance.\n\n\n\n\nGenerating Export Files\n\n\nWhen working with an ExportBuilder, users have the ability to generate export file formats from scratch, which can then be saved for later use as needed.  Alternately, export files can be generated from previously saved export formats, as well as from report formats saved when using the corresponding ReportBuilder.  To generate an export file using any of these methods - each of which is described here - complete the following steps:\n\n\nThe basic process of generating an export file is the same across all ExportBuilders; however, some steps may vary slightly based on the type of data being worked with and the ReportBuilder through which the ExportBuilder has been accessed.  Such variations are noted in the instructions that follow.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nOpen the ReportBuilder corresponding to the ExportBuilder you would like to use. (The steps that follow use the Demographics ExportBuilder, accessed from the \nReports\n module, as an example.)\n\n\n\n\n\n\nOn the initial \nReport Settings\n page that is displayed, use the \nDate Range\n \noption(s) on this page to limit the export file&#39;s contents by data date and/or client roster dates. When doing so please keep in mind that in all ExportBuilders (with the exception of the HMIS History ExportBuilder) a two year period of your choosing is the maximum length of time for which an export can be generated.  Also be aware that the date range(s) specified here are not included in saved export formats and must be set for each export generated.\n\n\nThe specific options you see are based on the ReportBuilder through which you have accessed this ExportBuilder; both utilize the same date range settings.  As a result, it may be useful to review the instructions for the relevant ReportBuilder if you have questions about the date range setting(s) available here.\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nIf you are working with a client-centric ReportBuilder/ExportBuilder, the \nClient \noption on this page is used to determine which client records are to be included in the export.  Available options are:\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClients with Records\n - The default.  Includes in the export all clients with records matching the export settings.  Any client WITHOUT records matching the export settings is excluded.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nAll Client\ns\n - Includes in the export all clients on the program roster, whether or not they have records that match the export settings.  Those without matches will display &#34;--&#34; for data variables in the export where no match exists.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nSelect Client\n \n- Limits export contents to those records for a single client only.  Use the corresponding drop-down (automatically displayed when &#34;Select Client&#34; is clicked) to choose the client for whom the export is to be generated.  (This allows you to easily generate exports, including saved exports, for a single client without having to use the filter options later on in the export generation process.)\n\n\n\n\n\n\nLeave this selection at its default value, or adjust by clicking one of the other radio buttons if needed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick the \nProvide ExportBuilder Options\n checkbox.  \nThe page is updated to reflect your choice, changing to \nExport Setting\ns\n, and displaying relevant export format selection options at the bottom of the page\n\n\n\n\n\n\nIf you would like the export file (along with a copy of the error log, when applicable) to be sent to your AWARDS Messages module upon its completion, click the\n Send in AWARDS Message\n checkbox.  When this option is selected, you need not wait for the export to generate, and can navigate away from the ExportBuilder after finishing the configuration process - this can be a time-saver, especially when working with exports containing large amounts of data that might otherwise take a while to display in AWARDS.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhen using this option the export file will remain in your messages for one month, after which time it will be deleted.  (The exact date of deletion will be displayed in red at the top of the report message for your reference.)  If you know you will need the export content after that time, be sure to download the file attached to the export message.  If you don&#39;t download the attachment before the message is deleted, you will need to manually re-generate the export if you need access to it in the future.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nUse the export format drop-down options at the bottom of the page to specify whether you will be generating the export file from scratch or using a previously saved format.  Available choices are to:\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nUse a previously saved export format\n - To generate the export file using a format previously created and saved using this ExportBuilder, select the format from the first drop-down.  (The page is updated to remove the second drop-down, which is not applicable in this case.)  When using this method, you can immediately generate the export file without altering any settings.  Alternately, you can first review and modify the settings of the format by checking off the available Provide option to modify settings of saved export format checkbox.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nUse a previously saved ReportBuilder report format\n - To generate the export file based on a report format previously created and saved using the corresponding ReportBuilder, leave the first drop-down blank, and select the report format from the second drop-down.  When using this method, data variable selections and content/order settings in the steps that follow will default from those used in the selected report, and can be adjusted as needed.  Export file format and field mapping settings must be manually set.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nAny changes made to the default settings of a report format in the steps that follow are only applicable to the export file being generated.  To make changes to the report format itself that are then saved with that format, you must do so through the corresponding ReportBuilder.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nStart from scratch, without using a previously saved export or report format \n- To create your own export format for single-time use or for saving and later re-use, leave both drop-down options blank.  When using this method, the steps that follow will include selection of data variables, and the setting of options for data content/order, file formatting, and field mapping.\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick \nCONTINUE\n.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nIf you are using an existing export format and have NOT chosen to view/modify the settings of that format, you are now taken directly to the export file page.  Continue with step \n21\n.\n\n\nIn all other instances, the \nOptions \npage is displayed.  Continue with step \n8\n.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nConfigure the options on this page as follows, or make changes to existing selections as needed:\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhen working with an existing report or export format the settings on this page default to those previously saved with that format.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nUnduplicated Client Count\n \n- \nBy\n default some export files will include all clients who were enrolled in the selected program(s) at some point during the date range specified in step \n2\n.  That may result in duplicate records in instances where a client has multiple histories in a single program, or a history in multiple programs.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhen applicable to the current ExportBuilder, you will see an \nUnduplicated Client Count\ncheckbox at the top of the page.  Check off that option if you would like to filter out any duplicates, list any client only once in the export file, and limit file contents to the most recent data for each included client.\n\n\nThis option is not available/relevant for all ExportBuilders, based on the type of data being worked with.  If you do not see this checkbox, please disregard and continue on to choosing the fields to display.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nChoose fields to display\n \n- \nThe \nSettings\npage contains a list of data variables related to the ExportBuilder you are working with, representing data collected in various AWARDS locations.  Click the checkbox next to each variable to be included in the export file, and/or use the available\n \nCheck All\noption(s) as needed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nThe specific data variables you see are based on the ReportBuilder through which you have accessed this ExportBuilder; both utilize the same options on this page.  As a result, it may be useful to review the instructions for the relevant ReportBuilder if you have questions about the data variable selections.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick \nCONTINUE\n.  The\n \nOptions - Continued \npage is displayed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nConfigure the options on this page as follows to adjust the scope and order of export file contents, or make changes to any default settings as needed:\nWhen working with an existing report or export format, the settings on this page default to those previously saved with that format.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nFilter Options\n \n-\n \nFilters apply user-specified criteria to the export file variables chosen on the previous page, thereby limiting included records.  To set a filter, click the drop-down arrow on the left in the first filter options row and select the variable to be used for filtering.  Click the corresponding drop-down arrow and select the type of filter to be used.  Lastly, in the corresponding field to the\n \nright of the filter selection, type the filter criteria.  For example, these three columns could be set as &#34;Gender,&#34; &#34;Equals,&#34; and &#34;Males&#34; to limit file contents to only those records for male clients.\n\n\n\n\nRepeat as needed for a total of up to eight filters.  (After adding four filters, click the \nAdd Additional Filter \nlink to add others.)  If you have added multiple filters, be sure to click the \nand \nor \nor \nradio button to specify how those filters should be combined.  The default is &#34;and.&#34;\n\n\nIt is not possible to combine &#34;and&#34; and &#34;or&#34; filters.  As a result, the filter combination selection made here applies to all filters set for the export.\n\n\n\n\nWith Record Numbers\n \n-\n \nIf the rows of data in the export file should be numbered, click this checkbox.\n\n\n\n\nSort By / Order\n \n-\n \nTo sort the export file by one or more of the included data variables, click the\n \nSort By\n \ndrop-down arrow(s) and select the variable(s).  Click the corresponding\n \nOrder\n \ndrop-down arrow for each sorting selection to indicate whether the sort is to be ascending (&#34;A-Z&#34;) or descending (&#34;Z-A&#34;).\n\n\n\n\n\n\nRepeat as needed.  Up to four sort by/order selections can be configured initially, after which additional selections can be added using the\n \nAdd Additional Sorting\n \nlink.  Using that link you can add as many sort by/order selections as there are data variables chosen for inclusion in the ExportBuilder.\n\n\n\n\nField Display Order\n \n-\n \nTo\n \nadjust the order of the data columns in the export file, click a variable in the &#34;Field Display Order&#34; portion of the page to highlight it.  Use the up and down arrow icons to the right of the variables list to move the highlighted variable up or down in the list, or drag and drop the variable to the desired position.  Repeat as needed.  The field display order listed from top to bottom here will be reflected in the export file columns display from left to right.\n\n\n\n\nIf more than eight data variables were selected on the previous page, the variables list is not automatically displayed.  You must first click \nShow Ordered Field List\n before setting the order.\n\n\nTo hide a field from the export contents entirely, move it beneath the available &#34;Hidden Fields&#34; row.  By doing so you can still filter your export contents on that variable, but keep its individual detail from being a part of the export if it is not necessary to include it.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick\n \nCONTINUE TO EXPORT SETTINGS\n.  A second\n \nOptions - Continued\npage is displayed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\n  When working with an existing export format, the settings on this page default to those saved with that format.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick the\n \nExport Type \ndrop-down arrow and select the type of export file you are generating:  CSV, TXT, XLS, or XML.  \nThe page is updated based on your selection.\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nOnly export and data options applicable to the format you&#39;ve chosen are displayed on the page, and may include a combination of the following:\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nHeader Row\n\n\n\n\n\n\nFixed Width / Delimited By\n\n\n\n\n\n\nFields Qualified By\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nText Padding\n\n\n\n\n\n\nNumeric Padding\n\n\n\n\n\n\nRoot Node\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nContainer Node\n\n\n\n\n\n\nBoolean Values\n\n\n\n\n\n\nDate Format / Separator\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nList Delimiter\n\n\n\n\n\n\nPhone Format\n\n\n\n\n\n\nValue Mapping\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n  Configure or make changes to the settings of the applicable options as needed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick\n \nCONTINUE TO EXPORT SETTINGS\n.  The final\n \nOptions - Continued\n \npage is displayed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhen working with an existing export format, the settings on this page default to those saved with that format.\nData fields listed from top to bottom of this page represent export file fields/columns as they will be included from left to right.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nTo add placeholder fields to the export file for the purposes of inserting a sub-export, accommodating export fields that are not available in AWARDS, or adding an AWARDS data field multiple times, or to remove a previously added placeholder or data field, use the icons to the left of the\n \nData\n \ncolumn:\n\n\n\n\n\n\n &#34;Sub-exports&#34; allow for the \nembedding of saved export formats from other ExportBuilders.  Those export formats link data for each client from the corresponding ExportBuilders to the Demographics ExportBuilder.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nAdd New Field\n \n-\n \nClick this icon to add a new field to export file to the left of the one in which the icon is clicked.  A\n \nSelect Field \nType\n \ndialog box is displayed.  Click the available drop-down arrow and choose the type of field being added:\n\n\n\n\n\n\nData Field\n \n- \nAdds an existing AWARDS field to the export.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nPlaceholder Field\n \n- \nAdds a new field that is not currently available in the export file.\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick\n \nOk\n \nto confirm your selection and display the updated settings page.  In the\n \nData\ncolumn for the newly added field, either select the field name (if you have added a data field), or enter a name for the field (if you have added a placeholder).\n\n\nIf you are working in the Demographics ExportBuilder and the new field is to contain a sub-export from another AWARDS module, be sure to select &#34;Export Format&#34; as the field type in step \n15\n.  You will then be able to select the sub-export&#39;s module and format name from the Data column rather than typing something in.\n\n\n\n\nCombine Field\n \n-\n \nClick this icon to combine the field with the row above it.\n\n\n\n\nThis option is useful for generating specific-use fields that an entity like a funder might ask for.  For example, one of the many ways in which this feature can be used is in an instance where a funder requires a field to start with leading characters.  In such cases you could use the \nAdd New\n icon to add in a placeholder for those characters, and then combine it, using the combine option, with the field with which those characters should be associated.\n\n\n\n\nSplit Fields\n \n-\n \nClick this icon to split previously combined fields back into their original component parts.\n\n\n\n\nWhen non-placeholder fields, or field combinations without placeholders in them, are broken apart, the labels for the component fields return to their original values.\n\n\n\n\nDelete Field\n \n-\n \nClick this icon to remove a placeholder field.\n\n\n\n\nIn order to delete a placeholder field which has been combined with another field, you must first split those two fields into their components using the \nSplit\n option detailed above.\n\n\n\n\nFor each of the &#34;Data&#34; (and placeholder) fields displayed, configure the fields and options on this page as follows:\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nField Name\n \n-\n \nTo use a field name other than the one specified on this page, overwrite the existing field name.  This change applies to the export file only, and is not reflected elsewhere in AWARDS.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nField Width (Start - Finish)\n \n-\n \nIn this box\n, type the column width this field should occupy in the export file.  The start and finish positions are automatically determined based on the specified width for this field and any previous fields.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nThis option is only available for the TXT export type, when &#34;Fixed Width&#34; was selected on the previous page.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nField Type\n \n-\n \nClick this drop-down arrow\n \nand change the field type if needed.  Available options are &#34;Text,&#34; &#34;Num\neric,&#34; &#34;Date,&#34; &#34;Time.&#34; &#34;Date/Time,&#34; &#34;Yes/No,&#34;  &#34;List,&#34; and &#34;Phone.&#34;\n\n\n\n\n\n\nThe field type specified here is used in conjunction with the padding options specified on the previous page, to ensure that the export file data is formatted appropriately.\n&#34;Export Format&#34; -\nThis option is only available for the XML export type \nand when working in the Demographics ExportBuilder if the system finds a saved export format of the same export type in one of the following locations:\n- Hospital &gt; Episodes\n- Medical &gt; Allergies\n- Medical &gt; Medications\n- Employment &gt; Job Placements\n- Employment &gt; Job Interviews\nIf &#34;Export Format&#34; is selected here, the Data column for this variable will include two drop-down options.  Use those options to select the module and export format to be used as a sub-export.  When the export file is generated, data from the sub-export will be included. \n\n\n\n\nTag or Property\n \n-\n \nClick\n \nthis drop-down arrow and make a selection to indicate whether the export variable is a &#34;Tag,&#34; &#34;Sub-Tag,&#34; or &#34;Property.&#34;  The default value is &#34;Tag.&#34;\n\n\n\n\nThis option is only available for the XML export type.\nA property applies to the closest tag or sub-tag that precedes it, or to the container node when working with the variable at the top of the list.  (An exception is made if the first variable is a sub-export, in which case the sub-export, as well as the very next field, will be treated as a tag, regardless of what is selected from this drop-down.)\nA sub-tag will be indented under the closest tag that precedes it.  The tag will be closed before the next field identified as a tag, or at the end of the file if there are no more tags that follow.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nRequired\n \n-\n \nIf data is\n \nto be required in this field, click the corresponding checkbox (or use the\n \nCheck All\n \noption at the top of the page to require all fields in the file).  If any required fields do not have values in them when the export file is generated, those fields will be flagged in an error file.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nValues Mapping\n \n-\n \nIf any\n \nfield values should be mapped to a different value during the export process, click the corresponding checkbox.  Three sets of\n \nCriteria\n,\n \nFrom Value\n, \n \nand\n \nTo Value\n \nfields are displayed. \n\n\n\n\n\n\nAvailable\n \nCriteria\n \nselections are &#34;Equals,&#34; &#34;Not Equal to,&#34; &#34;Greater than,&#34; &#34;Greater than or Equal to,&#34; &#34;Less than,&#34; &#34;Less than or Equal to,&#34; &#34;Contains,&#34; &#34;Does not Contain,&#34; &#34;Is Blank,&#34; &#34;Is not Blank,&#34; and &#34;Between.&#34; The default value is &#34;Equals.&#34;\n\n\nAny entry in the\n \nFrom Value\n \nfield will appear as its corresponding\n \nTo Value\n \nentry. \n\n\nAt least one\n \nCriteria/From/To\n \nset must be entered if the &#34;Values Mapping&#34; option is selected.  If more Criteria/From/To sets are needed, click the\n \nAdd Additional Mapping\n \nlink. \n \nTo delete a mapping value set, click the red\n \nDelete\n \nicon to the right of the row to be removed from the Values Mapping table. \n\n\nThe\n \nValue Mapping\n \nfield on the second\n \nOptions\n \npage, displaying &#34;Values not specified should remain as is:&#34; and a checkbox is related to this functionality.  If this is left unchecked, any values not listed will appear blank in the export file and on the screen display.  If it is checked, they will remain as they appear in the ReportBuilder.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nTo save the settings made on this and the previous page so that this export file can easily be re-generated in the future, click\n \nSAVE EXPORT FORMAT\n.  The\n \nSave Export Format \ndialog box is displayed.\nIf it is not necessary to save the export format and you only want to generate the export file, skip to step \n22\n.  Please keep in mind before doing so that if you choose to generate the export file without first saving, you may lose new fields added to the export if you later jump back to save the format.  If you need to retain the full export format, always save here during step \n17.\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nIn the\n \nExport Format Title \nfield, type the name of the format.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhen working with a previously saved export format, please keep in mind that only the user who originally saved a format and individuals who can save formats for all users can modify that format.  Others can save modified formats under a new name, but cannot apply the modifications to the original format.\nWe recommend that the title used be indicative of the export contents so that it is easily identifiable when selecting the format for the purposes of generating the export file in the future.\n\n\n\n\nIn the\n \nDescription \nfield, type or make changes to a brief description of the saved format.\nAt present any descriptions entered using this new field will be for reference purposes in the individual ExportBuilder only; however, in the future this data will be a valuable component of the highly anticipated Saved Report Format ReportBuilder to be made available in a future product release.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick one of the available\n \nS\nave Export Format\n \nfor\n \nradio buttons to determine whom the export format should be accessible to.  Available options are:\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nYourself\n \n- \nWhen selected, only the user saving the export format has the ability to use or modify that format.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nAll Staff\n - \nWhen selected, all staff in the selected program(s) have the ability to use the export format; however, only you and other individuals with the ability to save export formats for all staff will be able\n to modify or delete that format.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nUsers with the \nSave ReportBuilder Formats For All Staff \npermission (assigned under System Setup &gt; \nPermissions Maintenance\n) have the ability to save export formats to be used by others.  Users without this permission can only save export formats for themselves.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick\n \nSave Export Format\n.  The export format is saved and the\n \nOptions - Continued \npage is re-displayed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick\n \nGENERATE EXPORT FILE\n.  The export file is generated and the\n \nExportBuilder\n \npage is displayed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nIf you chose the &#34;Send in AWARDS Message&#34; option on the initial export option settings page, a notice will be displayed upon clicking GENERATE EXPORT FILE to let you know that the export file will be sent to your\n \nMessages\n \nmodule upon completion.  You can then navigate away from the page, or wait for the export to complete and then proceed with step \n23\n.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nAt this time you can:\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nReview the export contents\n \n-\n \nA report version of the export file contents is displayed on the\n \nExportBuilder\n \npage.  The data in that report can be sorted and worked with on the page as needed, though keep in mind that any changes made here are not applied to the export file itself.\n\n\n\n\nView the error log\n \n-\n \nIf any errors were found when the export file was generated, and have been included in the export file, an error log link is available.  Use that log to review the errors (for example, a required field that does not contain data) and go back into the relevant records in AWARDS to make corrections as needed.  Once corrections have been made the export file must be re-generated.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nError log files are in .TXT format and can be viewed and worked with from within any text editor; for example, Notepad.\n\n\n\n\nDownload the export file\n \n-\n \nTo do so click the\n \nDownload File \nlink and follow the prompts on the subsequent dialog boxes to save that file to your computer. Included among these prompts is a\n \nPHI Download Alert\n, reminding you of your obligation to properly handle any confidential data and/or protected health information (PHI) within the export file in accordance with related government regulations and agency policies.  After reading the alert, click\n \nOK \nto acknowledge it and proceed with the download, or\n \nCancel\n \nto return to the previous page.\n\n\n\n\nThe process of generating an export file is now complete.\n\n\n\n\nFrequently Asked Questions\n\n\nYou may also find it helpful to reference the \nReportBuilders Frequently Asked Questions\n in addition to those provided below, as the ReportBuilder and ExportBuilder functionalities are similar in many respects.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nCan ExportBuilder formats be saved for specific programs?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nNo, export formats are designed to be saved for all programs.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nCan multiple ExportBuilder files be combined?\n\n\nWhen working with the Demographics ExportBuilder, a sub-export can b\ne included from one or more of the following modules if at least one export format of the same type has been saved there:\n\n\n\n\n\n\nHospital &gt; Episodes\n\n\n\n\n\n\nMedical &gt; Allergies\n\n\n\n\n\n\nMedical &gt; Medications\n\n\n\n\n\n\nEmployment &gt; Jobs &gt; Job Placements\n\n\n\n\n\n\nEmployment &gt; Jobs &gt; Job Interviews\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nTo add a sub-export to your Demographics export file, use the \nAdd New Field\n icon available on the \nOptions - Continued \npage where field mapping takes place.  From the field type selection list for that new field, select \nExport Format\n, and then in the &#34;Data&#34; column for that field select the relevant module and export format from the two available drop-down options.\n\n\nThe &#34;Export Format&#34;\n \noption is only available if the system recognizes that there is a saved export format in one of the locations listed above, and that the export format is in the XML type and which has to the be same type as the one you are currently working with.\n\n\nThe report version of the export file that is displayed when GENERATE EXPORT FILE is clicked will include a placeholder column for the sub-export data.  The actual sub-export data will be included in the export file itself.\n\n\nAt present, the combining of ExportBuilder files is limited to the specific case described here.  To combine other exports you can generate multiple ExportBuilder files and then piece them together outside of AWARDS, as needed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nHow do I delete a saved export format?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nA saved export format can be deleted if it is one you originally created.  If you have the ability to save export formats for other users, you also have the option to delete the format even if it is on you did not personally create.  To do so in either instance, complete the following steps:\n\n\n\n\n\n\nOpen the ReportBuilder corresponding to the ExportBuilder with which the format was originally saved.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nOn the initial \nReport Settings \npage, click the \nProvide ExportBuilder Options\n \ncheckbox\n.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nFrom the first export format drop-down option at the bottom of the page, select the format to be deleted.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick the \nProvide option to modify settings of saved export format \ncheckbox.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick \nCONTINUE\n.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick \nCONTINUE \nagain.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick \nCONTINUE TO EXPORT SETTINGS\n.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick \nCONTINUE TO EXPORT SETTINGS \nagain.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nClick \nDELETE EXPORT FORMAT\n at the bottom of the page.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nComplete the deletion process by clicking \nOK \non the confirmation dialog box that is then displayed.\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhen are FormBuilder fields available for inclusion in an export file?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nIn addition to the types of information available in each ExportBuilder, ExportBuilder data files may also include information entered in FormBuilder fields.  In order for a FormBuilder field to be available as a variable when generating ExportBuilder files, that field must have been configured for inclusion on related ReportBuilders.  Which ReportBuilder (and corresponding ExportBuilder) is &#34;related&#34; to a given form field is based on the form&#39;s location.  For example, if a form is available for inclusion when writing progress notes, the form&#39;s fields (when set to do so) will be available for inclusion when using the Progress Notes ReportBuilder, and therefore when using the Progress Notes ExportBuilder.  Click \nhere\n for more information on configuring form fields using the FormBuilder.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhich export / data options apply to which export types?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nOn the page on which your export type is chosen, the export and data options are updated based on that selection so that only those that are applicable are displayed.  The combination of available options for each export type is as follows\n:\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nField / other users?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nUsers with the \nSave ReportBuilder Formats For All Staff\n permission (assigned under System Setup &gt; \nPermissions Maintenance\n) have the ability to save export formats to be used by others.  Users without this permission can only save export formats for themselves.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhy can&#39;t I generate an ExportBuilder file for a date range of more than two years?\n\n\n\n\n\n\n \n\n\nIn order to maintain a high level of system performance for all AWARDS users, each ExportBuilder in AWARDS (with the exception of the HMIS History ExportBuilder) is limited to being run for a two year period of your choice.  If it&#39;s necessary for you to generate data for a longer period of time we recommend generating multiple exports for consecutive date ranges, and then copying and pasting the data from each into a single file.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhy doesn&#39;t anything happen when I click a link to download a file or attachment from AWARDS?\n\n\n\n\n\n\n \n\n\nClicking a link in AWARDS to download a report/export file, message attachment or Client File Cabinet document is designed to generate a &#34;PHI Download Alert&#34; pop-up notification to inform you that the content of the download may contain confidential or protected information and should be handled accordingly.  If you have a pop-up blocker turned on in your browser&#39;s settings and have not exempted *.footholdtechnology.com so that it is always allowed to open pop-ups, it will seem as if nothing is happening when the AWARDS link is clicked.  Please see the instructions for your browser to adjust the settings related to the pop-up blocker.\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhy is a change I made to a client&#39;s record not immediately reflected in an ExportBuilder file?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nThere are two possible causes when ExportBuilder files are not immediately displaying information updated in the same database login session.  First, it may be indicative of a memory cache issue.  To get around this, press \n&lt;CTRL+R&gt;\n while viewing the report page on which the export file link is displayed, or click your browser&#39;s refresh icon.  If the report content changes, re-generate the export file.\n\n\nSecond, when using some ExportBuilders (such as the HMIS or Demographics ExportBuilders) if the data file&#39;s results include fewer than 5,000 records, the data comes from the live database and should reflect what&#39;s currently in records.  If the data file&#39;s results include more than 5,000 records though, the data comes from the previous night&#39;s database backup, and it is only exported to the data file rather than displayed on the screen.  As a result, any changes made to records between the time of the backup and when the file was generated will not be reflected in the data.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false}}

once in the export file, and limit file contents to the most recent data for each included client.\n   333→\n   334→\n   335→This option is not available/relevant for all ExportBuilders, based on the type of data being worked with.  If you do not see this checkbox, please disregard and continue on to choosing the fields to display.\n   336→\n   337→\n   338→\n   339→\n   340→\n   341→\n   342→Choose fields to display\n   343→ \n   344→- \n   345→The \n   346→Settings\n   347→page contains a list of data variables related to the ExportBuilder you are working with, representing data collected in various AWARDS locations.  Click the checkbox next to each variable to be included in the export file, and/or use the available\n   348→ \n   349→Check All\n   350→option(s) as needed.\n   351→\n   352→\n   353→\n   354→\n   355→\n   356→\n   357→The specific data variables you see are based on the ReportBuilder through which you have accessed this ExportBuilder; both utilize the same options on this page.  As a result, it may be useful to review the instructions for the relevant ReportBuilder if you have questions about the data variable selections.\n   358→\n   359→\n   360→\n   361→\n   362→\n   363→\n   364→Click \n   365→CONTINUE\n   366→.  The\n   367→ \n   368→Options - Continued \n   369→page is displayed.\n   370→\n   371→\n   372→\n   373→\n   374→\n   375→\n   376→\n   377→\n   378→Configure the options on this page as follows to adjust the scope and order of export file contents, or make changes to any default settings as needed:\n   379→When working with an existing report or export format, the settings on this page default to those previously saved with that format.\n   380→\n   381→\n   382→\n   383→\n   384→\n   385→\n   386→Filter Options\n   387→ \n   388→-\n   389→ \n   390→Filters apply user-specified criteria to the export file variables chosen on the previous page, thereby limiting included records.  To set a filter, click the drop-down arrow on the left in the first filter options row and select the variable to be used for filtering.  Click the corresponding drop-down arrow and select the type of filter to be used.  Lastly, in the corresponding field to the\n   391→ \n   392→right of the filter selection, type the filter criteria.  For example, these three columns could be set as &#34;Gender,&#34; &#34;Equals,&#34; and &#34;Males&#34; to limit file contents to only those records for male clients.\n   393→\n   394→\n   395→\n   396→\n   397→Repeat as needed for a total of up to eight filters.  (After adding four filters, click the \n   398→Add Additional Filter \n   399→link to add others.)  If you have added multiple filters, be sure to click the \n   400→and \n   401→or \n   402→or \n   403→radio button to specify how those filters should be combined.  The default is &#34;and.&#34;\n   404→\n   405→\n   406→It is not possible to combine &#34;and&#34; and &#34;or&#34; filters.  As a result, the filter combination selection made here applies to all filters set for the export.\n   407→\n   408→\n   409→\n   410→\n   411→With Record Numbers\n   412→ \n   413→-\n   414→ \n   415→If the rows of data in the export file should be numbered, click this checkbox.\n   416→\n   417→\n   418→\n   419→\n   420→Sort By / Order\n   421→ \n   422→-\n   423→ \n   424→To sort the export file by one or more of the included data variables, click the\n   425→ \n   426→Sort By\n   427→ \n   428→drop-down arrow(s) and select the variable(s).  Click the corresponding\n   429→ \n   430→Order\n   431→ \n   432→drop-down arrow for each sorting selection to indicate whether the sort is to be ascending (&#34;A-Z&#34;) or descending (&#34;Z-A&#34;).\n   433→\n   434→\n   435→\n   436→\n   437→\n   438→\n   439→Repeat as needed.  Up to four sort by/order selections can be configured initially, after which additional selections can be added using the\n   440→ \n   441→Add Additional Sorting\n   442→ \n   443→link.  Using that link you can add as many sort by/order selections as there are data variables chosen for inclusion in the ExportBuilder.\n   444→\n   445→\n   446→\n   447→\n   448→Field Display Order\n   449→ \n   450→-\n   451→ \n   452→To\n   453→ \n   454→adjust the order of the data columns in the export file, click a variable in the &#34;Field Display Order&#34; portion of the page to highlight it.  Use the up and down arrow icons to the right of the variables list to move the highlighted variable up or down in the list, or drag and drop the variable to the desired position.  Repeat as needed.  The field display order listed from top to bottom here will be reflected in the export file columns display from left to right.\n   455→\n   456→\n   457→\n   458→\n   459→If more than eight data variables were selected on the previous page, the variables list is not automatically displayed.  You must first click \n   460→Show Ordered Field List\n   461→ before setting the order.\n   462→\n   463→\n   464→To hide a field from the export contents entirely, move it beneath the available &#34;Hidden Fields&#34; row.  By doing so you can still filter your export contents on that variable, but keep its individual detail from being a part of the export if it is not necessary to include it.\n   465→\n   466→\n   467→\n   468→\n   469→\n   470→\n   471→Click\n   472→ \n and format name from the Data column rather than typing something in.\n   726→\n   727→\n   728→\n   729→\n   730→Combine Field\n   731→ \n   732→-\n   733→ \n   734→Click this icon to combine the field with the row above it.\n   735→\n   736→\n   737→\n   738→\n   739→This option is useful for generating specific-use fields that an entity like a funder might ask for.  For example, one of the many ways in which this feature can be used is in an instance where a funder requires a field to start with leading characters.  In such cases you could use the \n   740→Add New\n   741→ icon to add in a placeholder for those characters, and then combine it, using the combine option, with the field with which those characters should be associated.\n   742→\n   743→\n   744→\n   745→\n   746→Split non-placeholder fields, or field combinations without placeholders in them, are broken apart, the labels for the component fields return to their original values.\n   756→\n   757→\n   758→\n   759→\n Employment &gt; Job Interviews\n   838→If &#34;Export Format&#34; is selected here, the Data column for this variable will include two drop-down options.  Use those options to select the module and export format to be used as a sub-export.  When the export file is generated, data from the sub-export will be included. \n   839→\n   840→\n   841→\n   842→\n   843→Tag or Property\n   844→ \n   845→-\n   846→ \n   847→Click\n   848→ \n   849→this drop-down arrow and make a selection to indicate whether the export variable is a &#34;Tag,&#34; &#34;Sub-Tag,&#34; or &#34;Property.&#34;  The default value is &#34;Tag.&#34;\n   850→\n   851→\n   852→\n   853→\n   854→This option is only available for the XML export type.\n   855→A property applies to the closest tag or sub-tag that precedes it, or to the container node when working with the variable at the top of the list.  (An exception is made if the first variable is a sub-export, in which case the sub-export, as well as the very next field, will be treated as a tag, regardless of what is selected from this drop-down.)\n   856→A sub-tag will be indented under the closest tag that precedes it.  The tag will be closed before the next field identified as a tag, or at the end of the file if there are no more tags that follow.\n   857→\n   858→\n   859→\n   860→\n   861→\n   862→\n   863→Required\n   864→ \n   865→-\n require all fields in the file).  If any required fields do not have values in them when the export file is generated, those fields will be flagged in an error file.\n   874→\n   875→\n   876→\n   877→\n   878→\n   879→\n   880→Values Mapping\n   881→ \n   882→-\n   883→ \n   884→If any\n   885→ \n   886→field values should be mapped to a different value during the export process, click the than,&#34; &#34;Greater than or Equal to,&#34; &#34;Less than,&#34; &#34;Less than or Equal to,&#34; &#34;Contains,&#34; &#34;Does not Contain,&#34; &#34;Is Blank,&#34; &#34;Is not Blank,&#34; and &#34;Between.&#34; The default value is &#34;Equals.&#34;\n   910→\n   911→\n   912→Any entry in the\n   913→ \n   914→From Value\n   915→ \n   916→field will appear as its corresponding\n   917→ \n on the second\n   945→ \n   946→Options\n   947→ \n   948→page, displaying &#34;Values not specified should remain as is:&#34; and a checkbox is related to this functionality.  If this is left unchecked, any values not listed will appear blank in the export file and on the screen display.  If it is checked, they will remain as they appear in the ReportBuilder.\n   949→\n   950→\n   951→\n   952→\n   953→\n   954→\n   955→To save the settings made on this and the previous page so that this export file can easily be re-generated in the future, click\n   956→ \n   957→SAVE EXPORT FORMAT\n 1093→\n  1094→\n  1095→\n  1096→\n  1097→\n  1098→\n  1099→Review the export contents\n  1100→ \n  1101→-\n  1102→ \n  1103→A report version of the export file contents is displayed on the\n  1104→ \n  1105→ExportBuilder\n  1106→ \n  1107→page.  The data in that report can be sorted and worked with on the page as needed, though keep in mind that any changes made here are not applied to the export file itself.\n  1108→\n  1109→\n  1110→\n  1111→\n  1112→View the error log\n  1113→ \n  1114→-\n  1115→ \n  1116→If any errors were found when the export file was generated, and have been included in the export file, an error log link is available.  Use that log to review the errors (for example, a required field that does not contain data) and go back into the relevant records in AWARDS to make corrections as needed.  Once corrections have been made the export file must be re-generated.\n  1117→\n  1118→\n  1119→\n  1120→\n  1121→\n  1122→\n  1123→Error log files are in .TXT format and can be viewed and worked with from within any text editor; for example, Notepad.\n  1124→\n  1125→\n  1126→\n  1127→\n  1128→Download the export file\n  1129→ \n  1130→-\n  1131→ \n  1132→To do so click the\n  1133→ \n  1134→Download File \n  1135→link and follow the prompts on the subsequent dialog boxes to save that file to your computer. Included among these prompts is a\n  1136→ \n  1137→PHI Download Alert\n  1138→, reminding you of your obligation to properly handle any confidential data and/or protected health information (PHI) within the export file in accordance with related government regulations and agency policies.  After reading the alert, click\n  1139→ \n  1140→OK \n  1141→to acknowledge it and proceed with the download, or\n  1142→ \n  1143→Cancel\n  1144→ \n  1145→to return to the previous page.\n  1146→\n  1147→\n  1148→\n  1149→\n  1150→The process of generating an export file is now complete.\n  1151→\n  1152→\n  1153→\n  1154→\n  1155→Frequently Asked Questions\n  1156→\n  1157→\n  1158→You may also find it helpful to reference the \n  1159→ReportBuilders Frequently Asked Questions\n  1160→ in formats be saved for specific programs?\n  1168→\n  1169→\n  1170→\n  1171→\n  1172→\n  1173→\n  1174→No, export formats are designed to be saved for all programs.\n  1175→\n  1176→\n  1177→\n 1724→Who can save ExportBuilder formats for other users?\n  1725→\n  1726→\n  1727→\n  1728→\n  1729→\n  1730→\n  1731→Users with the \n  1732→Save ReportBuilder Formats For All Staff\n export formats for themselves.\n  1736→\n  1737→\n  1738→\n  1739→\n  1740→\n  1741→\n  1742→Why can&#39;t I generate an ExportBuilder file for a date range of more than two years?\n  1743→\n  1744→\n  1745→\n  1746→\n  1747→\n  1748→\n  1749→ \n  1750→\n  1751→\n  1752→In order to maintain a high level of system performance for all AWARDS users, each ExportBuilder in AWARDS (with the exception of the HMIS History ExportBuilder) is limited to being run for a two year period of your choice.  If it&#39;s necessary for you to generate data for a longer period of time we recommend generating multiple exports for consecutive date ranges, and then copying and pasting the data from each into a single file.\n  1753→\n  1754→\n  1755→\n  1756→\n  1757→\n  1758→\n  1759→Why doesn&#39;t anything happen when I click a link to download a file or attachment from AWARDS?\n  1760→\n  1761→\n  1762→\n  1763→\n  1764→\n  1765→\n  1766→ \n  1767→\n  1768→\n  1769→Clicking a link in AWARDS to download a report/export file, message attachment or Client File Cabinet document is designed to generate a &#34;PHI Download Alert&#34; pop-up notification to inform you that the content of the download may contain confidential or protected information and should be handled accordingly.  If you have a pop-up blocker turned on in your browser&#39;s settings and have not exempted *.footholdtechnology.com so that it is always allowed to open pop-ups, it will seem as if nothing is happening when the AWARDS link is clicked.  Please see the instructions for your browser to adjust the settings related to the pop-up blocker.\n  1770→\n  1771→\n  1772→\n  1773→\n  1774→\n  1775→\n  1776→Why is a change I made to a client&#39;s record not immediately reflected in an ExportBuilder file?\n  1777→\n  1778→\n  1779→\n  1780→\n  1781→\n  1782→\n  1783→There are two possible causes when ExportBuilder files are not immediately displaying information updated in the same database login session.  First, it may be indicative of a memory cache issue.  To get around this, press \n  1784→&lt;CTRL+R&gt;\n  1785→ while viewing the report page on which the export file link is displayed, or click your browser&#39;s refresh icon.  If the report content changes, re-generate the export file.\n  1786→\n  1787→\n  1788→Second, when using some ExportBuilders (such as the HMIS or Demographics ExportBuilders) if the data file&#39;s results include fewer than 5,000 records, the data comes from the live database and should reflect what&#39;s currently in records.  If the data file&#39;s results include more than 5,000 records though, the data comes from the previous night&#39;s database backup, and it is only exported to the data file rather than displayed on the screen.  As a result, any changes made to records between the time of the backup and when the file was generated will not be reflected in the data.\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware.
